## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 196 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安底阿戈</rb><rt>En-ti-ah-ko</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打發</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>欽差</rb><rt>kyʽing-tsʽa</rt><rb>過來</rb><rt>ko-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讓出</rb><rt>nyiang-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>安底阿戈</rb><rt>En-ti-ah-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>讓</rb><rt>nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>漢尼巴</rb><rt>Hen-nyi-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>趁此</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽ</rt><rb>機會</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>挑唆</rb><rt>tʽiao-so</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>打敗仔</rb><rt>Tang-ba-ts</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奪了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該管<sup>\[2\]</sup>了</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Antiochus，前一章譯作<u>安德約戈</u>，<u>塞琉古</u>帝國第六位國王<u>安條克</u>三世</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 統治；管轄</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>馬基頓</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>第五</rb><rt>di-ng</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>幫襯</rb><rt>pông-tsʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>敗仔</rb><rt>ba-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講和</rb><rt>kông-ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只得</rb><rt>tsih-teh</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>當頭</rb><rt>tông-deo</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抲去</rb><rt>kʽô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>個</rb><rt>Lo-mô-go</rt><rb>屬國了</rb><rt>joh-koh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 168 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Μακεδονία，現譯爲<u>馬其頓</u></sup>

<sup>\[2\] Φίλιππος Ε΄，此處爲<u>馬其頓</u>國王<u>腓力</u>五世</sup>

<sup>\[3\] 人質</sup>

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南邊</rb><rt>Nen-pin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>特買<sup>\[1\]</sup>個</rb><rt>deh-ma-go</rt><rb>小國</rb><rt>siao-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總名</rb><rt>cong-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞該亞</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>趁此</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽ</rt><rb>機會</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放火</rb><rt>fông-ho</rt><rb>燒掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同年個</rb><rt>dong-nyin-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>Tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西班牙</rb><rt>Si-pæn-yüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該管了</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby> 45 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿及爾</rb><rt>Ah-gyih-r</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[4\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>求搿泰</rb><rt>Gyiu-geh-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[5\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>謀殺了</rb><rt>meo-sah-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>救兵</rb><rt>kyiu-ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>殺掉了</rb><rt>sah-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>求搿泰</rb><rt>Gyiu-geh-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>餓</rb><rt>ngo</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該管個</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-go</rt><rb>屬國了</rb><rt>joh-koh-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 好些個；好幾個。現代寧波話說「特猛」</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Ἀχαΐα</sup>

<sup>\[3\] Κόρινθος，又譯爲<u>科林斯</u></sup>

<sup>\[4\] Algiers，現譯爲<u>阿爾及爾</u>，此處指北非古國<u>努米底亞</u>，在今<u>阿爾及利亞</u>北部</sup>

<sup>\[5\] Jugurtha，現譯爲<u>朱古達</u>。<u>努米底亞</u>國王</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>揚開</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三𡍲</rb><rt>sæn-tʽah</rt><rb>大洲</rb><rt>da-tsiu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>該管個</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄到</rb><rt>long-tao</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄到</rb><rt>long-tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>頭頂</rb><rt>deo-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>別國個</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>流出來</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>澆澆</rb><rt>kyiao-kyiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>田畈</rb><rt>din-pæn</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>外國</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>征服了</rb><rt>tsing-voh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勢道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>列王</rb><rt>lih-wông</rt><rb>廢王</rb><rt>fi-wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>本國</rb><rt>peng-koh</rt><rb>掌權</rb><rt>công-gyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>外國</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>殺了</rb><rt>sah-leh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>嘸數目賬個</rb><rt>m-su-moh-tsiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸要緊</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>阿及爾</rb><rt>Ah-gyih-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>打個</rb><rt>tang-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>元帥</rb><rt>nyün-sæ</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>出身</rb><rt>cʽih-sing</rt><rb>來勒</rb><rt>læ-leh</rt><rb>低微</rb><rt>ti-vi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>性格</rb><rt>sing-keh</rt><rb>兇惡</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>公子</rb><rt>kong-ts</rt><rb>出身</rb><rt>cʽih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>資質</rb><rt>ts-tsih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>功勞</rb><rt>kong-lao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>切齒</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽ</rt><rb>切恨</rb><rt>tsʽih-ʽeng</rt><rb>氣其弗過</rb><rt>kyʽi-gyi-feh-ko</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Gaius Marius，<u>羅馬</u>改革家</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Lucius Cornelius Sulla，現譯爲<u>蘇拉</u>。<u>羅馬</u>將軍、政治家、軍事家</sup>

<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>依了</rb><rt>i-deh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>諸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歸勒</rb><rt>kwe-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弄出</rb><rt>long-cʽih</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打贏仔</rb><rt>tang-ying-ts</rt><rb>仗</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>馬略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>充軍</rb><rt>cʽong-kyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>本都</rb><rt>Peng-tu</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米替大底</rb><rt>Mi-tʽi-da-ti</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>馬略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt><rb>起了</rb><rt>kyʽi-leh</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>破進</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一連</rb><rt>ih-lin</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>公侯</rb><rt>kong-ʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>嘸數目賬</rb><rt>m-su-moh-tsiang</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>弄權</rb><rt>long-gyün</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>公舉</rb><rt>kong-kyü</rt><rb>總管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇年</rb><rt>keh-nyin</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>喫醉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>潮熱</rb><rt>dziao-nyih</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Πόντος</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Μιθριδάτης，現譯爲<u>米特里達梯</u>，此處爲<u>本都</u>王國國王<u>米特里達梯</u>六世</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>馬略</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-liah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>贏仔</rb><rt>ying-ts</rt><rb>破進</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt><rb>城裏向</rb><rt>dzing-li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨氣</rb><rt>ün-kyʽi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>報仇</rb><rt>pao-dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺掉個</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>數都</rb><rt>su-tu</rt><rb>數弗遍</rb><rt>su-feh-pin</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弄權</rb><rt>long-gyün</rt><rb>弄到</rb><rt>long-tao</rt><rb>三年</rb><rt>sæn-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>讓位</rb><rt>nyiang-we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>白身</rb><rt>bah-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-Su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt></ruby> 80 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩句</rb><rt>liang-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>寫勒</rb><rt>sia-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>報恩</rb><rt>pao-eng</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>厚</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>庉晌</rb><rt>Deng-zông</rt><rb>幾年</rb><rt>kyi-nyin</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>大將</rb><rt>da-tsiang</rt><rb>掌權</rb><rt>công-gyün</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-dzông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>亞細亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>征服個</rb><rt>tsing-voh-go</rt><rb>國數</rb><rt>koh-su</rt><rb>統總</rb><rt>tʽong-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>破進</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt><rb>擄掠個</rb><rt>lo-liah-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>八百</rb><rt>pah-pah</rt><rb>多星</rb><rt>to-sing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>在其內</rb><rt>dzæ-gyi-nen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 63 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Gnaeus Pompeius，現譯爲<u>龐培</u>。<u>羅馬</u>軍事家、政治家</sup>

<ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>二個</rb><rt>nyi-go</rt><rb>大將</rb><rt>da-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>年代</rb><rt>nyin-dæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>西半邊</rb><rt>Si-pun-pin</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>打仗個</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-ko</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>西班牙</rb><rt>Si-pæn-yüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>佛蘭西</rb><rt>veh-læn-si</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>日耳曼</rb><rt>Jih-r-mæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>荷蘭</rb><rt>ʽO-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>粗蠻</rb><rt>tsʽu-mæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有勇</rb><rt>yiu-yüong</rt><rb>無謀</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>兵法</rb><rt>ping-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百多</rb><rt>ih-pah-to</rt><rb>萬</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擄掠來個</rb><rt>lo-liah-læ-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Gaius Julius Caesar，現譯爲<u>凱撒</u>。<u>羅馬</u>軍事家、政治家</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 現譯爲<u>法蘭西</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>葛萊蘇</rb><rt>Keh-læ-su</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>敘事</rb><rt>jü-z</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Marcus Licinius Crassus，現譯爲<u>克拉蘇</u>。<u>羅馬</u>軍事家、政治家</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 富有</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>葛萊蘇</rb><rt>Keh-læ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>敗仔</rb><rt>ba-ts</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>謀殺了</rb><rt>meo-sah-de</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>皇帝個</rb><rt>wông-ti-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>造反</rb><rt>dzao-fæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>弗上</rb><rt>feh-zông</rt><rb>四年</rb><rt>s-nyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>公侯</rb><rt>kong-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>兇手個</rb><rt>hyüong-siu-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>結了黨</rb><rt>kyih-deh-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 44 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>公侯</rb><rt>Kong-ʽeo</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>常</rb><rt>djông</rt><rb>常</rb><rt>djông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>戲文</rb><rt>hyi-veng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>擄掠來個</rb><rt>lo-liah-læ-go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>幫襯</rb><rt>pông-tsʽeng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>外甥</rb><rt>nga-sang</rt><rb>報其個</rb><rt>pao-gyi-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 厭惡</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 給予</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>外甥</rb><rt>nga-sang</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>屋大維</rb><rt>Oh-da-vi</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大將</rb><rt>da-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安敦</rb><rt>En-teng</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>結了黨</rb><rt>kyih-deh-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>阿妹</rb><rt>ah-me</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>公侯</rb><rt>kong-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安敦</rb><rt>En-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女王</rb><rt>nyü-wông</rt><rb>同室</rb><rt>dong-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>屋大維</rb><rt>Oh-da-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶領</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>安敦</rb><rt>En-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>敗仔</rb><rt>ba-ts</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女王</rb><rt>nyü-wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Gaius Octavius，<u>羅馬</u>帝國第一任元首</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Marcus Antonius，現譯爲<u>安東尼</u>，<u>羅馬</u>政治家、軍事家</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>屋大維</rb><rt>Oh-da-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爵位</rb><rt>tsiah-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>號</rb><rt>ʽao</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>奧古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>尊重個</rb><rt>tseng-djong-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>寄拜</rb><rt>kyi-pa</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爵位</rb><rt>tsiah-we</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>世傳個</rb><rt>shü-djün-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>屋大</u><sup>\[2\]</sup>就</rb><rt>Oh-da-ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>第一個</rb><rt>di-ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Augustus，現譯爲<u>奧古斯都</u></sup>

<sup>\[2\] 原文漏了一個「維」</sup>

### <ruby><rb>問個</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奪了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>馬基頓</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>亞該亞</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u>？<u><ruby><rb>阿及爾</rb><rt>Ah-gyih-r</rt></ruby></u>？<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>報應</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>馬略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>權</rb><rt>gyün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？

## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>廟</rb><rt>miao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>寂納<sup>\[1\]</sup>廟</rb><rt>Dzih-neh-miao</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>廟門</rb><rt>miao-meng</rt><rb>關了</rb><rt>kwæn-liao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>廟門</rb><rt>miao-meng</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>開個</rb><rt>kʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Janus，現譯爲<u>雅努斯</u>。<u>羅馬</u>宗教中的門神</sup>

<ruby><rb>自從</rb><rt>Z-dzong</rt><rb>建</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>loh-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算來</rb><rt>sön-læ</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 752 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>廟門</rb><rt>miao-meng</rt><rb>篤篤</rb><rt>toh-toh</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>關了</rb><rt>kwæn-leh</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>暫時</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>關一關</rb><rt>kwæn-ih-kwæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連忙</rb><rt>lin-mông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇勒</rb><rt>Keh-leh</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>逐節</rb><rt>djoh-tsih</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>篤發之頭</rb><rt>tôh-fah-ts-deo</rt><rb>本來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>鄞</u>縣</rb><rt>Nying-yün</rt><rb>介管</rb><rt>ka-kun</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>直等</rb><rt>Dzih-teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奧古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>北邊</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>冷冷</rb><rt>lang-lang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>火熱個</rb><rt>ho-nyih-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>西邊</rb><rt>si-pin</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>四近四遠</rb><rt>s-gying-s-yün</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>國打國</rb><rt>koh-tang-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>使喚</rb><rt>s-hwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby> 600 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>但也理</rb><rt>Dæn-Yiæ-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>四隻</rb><rt>s-tsah</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比倫</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>連牽</rb><rt>lin-kyʽin</rt><rb>興起來</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>第四</rb><rt>di-s</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兇色色</rb><rt>hyüong-seh-seh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勁道</rb><rt>gying-dao</rt><rb>比衆不同</rb><rt>pi-cong-peh-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴巴裏</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>呆</rb><rt>ngæ</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>鐵個</rb><rt>tʽih-go</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樣色</rb><rt>yiang-seh</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咬碎</rb><rt>ngao-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫剩個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>踏糊</rb><rt>dah-wu</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>章裏</rb><rt>tsông-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] 鄰近</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Daniel，現譯作<u>但以理</u>、<u>達尼爾</u>。《舊約聖經·但以理書》的作者</sup>

<sup>\[3\] 指《但以理書》第七章「四獸的異象」</sup>

<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>第四</rb><rt>di-s</rt><rb>章裏</rb><rt>tsông-li</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>呆大大</rb><rt>ngæ-do-do</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>手梗</rb><rt>siu-kwang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大腿</rb><rt>do-tʽe</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>菜花銅</rb><rt>tsʽæ-hwô-dong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腳梗</rb><rt>kyiah-kwang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>鐵</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>鐵</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>連牽</rb><rt>lin-kyʽin</rt><rb>興起來</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>）。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>鐵</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>強</rb><rt>gyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>鐵</rb><rt>Tʽih</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>敲碎</rb><rt>kʽao-se</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>抄滅</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>鐵</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>強橫</rb><rt>gyiang-wang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>併攏</rb><rt>ping-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>兩下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>膠粘</rb><rt>kao-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>鐵</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>所立失道</rb><rt>sô-lih-shih-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>和弗攏個</rb><rt>ʽo-feh-long-go</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] 指《但以理書》第二章「巨人像的異夢」</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>句</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>講完</rb><rt>kông-wun</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屬國</rb><rt>joh-koh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>猛洋洋</rb><rt>mang-yiang-yiang</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>雜七不剌</rb><rt>zeh-tsʽih-peh-la</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾候我弗齊</rb><rt>ng-ʽeo-ngô-feh-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>雖</rb><rt>se</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 浩瀚；廣大</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>耶</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt><rb>穌</rb><rt>su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>loh-væn</rt></ruby> 68 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿多</rb><rt>Ah-to</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>殺殺掉</rb><rt>sah-sah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一支</rb><rt>ih-ts</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>日耳曼</rb><rt>Jih-r-mæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿多</rb><rt>Ah-to</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>阿多</rb><rt>Ah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抵擋</rb><rt>ti-tông</rt><rb>弗住</rb><rt>feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>尋死</rb><rt>zing-si</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一支</rb><rt>ih-ts</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>國</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>風聲</rb><rt>fong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看樣</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>菲士巴三</rb><rt>Fi-z-pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>歸根</rb><rt>Kwe-keng</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>菲士巴三</rb><rt>Fi-z-pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打贏</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Marcus Salvius Otho，現譯爲<u>奧托</u>。<u>羅馬</u>帝國皇帝之一</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Titus Flavius Vespasianus，英語化爲 Vespasian，現譯爲<u>韋斯巴薌</u>。<u>羅馬</u>帝國<u>弗拉維</u>王朝的第一位皇帝</sup>

<sup>\[3\] 最終</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>loh-væn</rt></ruby> 193 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>羽林兵</rb><rt>yü-ling-ping</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>不蒂捺士</rb><rt>Peh-ti-nah-z</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做事體</rb><rt>tso-z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怏糟糟</rb><rt>ông-tsao-tsao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>皇帝個</rb><rt>wông-ti-go</rt><rb>爵位</rb><rt>tsiah-we</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>多個</rb><rt>to-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt></ruby> 900,0000 <ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>登位</rb><rt>teng-we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>做了</rb><rt>Tso-leh</rt><rb>五個月</rb><rt>ng-ko-yüih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>謀皇</rb><rt>meo-wông</rt><rb>篡位</rb><rt>tsʽön-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>太平個</rb><rt>tʽa-bing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>缺個</rb><rt>kyʽüih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>三百</rb><rt>Sæn-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>統總</rb><rt>tʽong-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒有</rb><rt>tao-yiu</rt><rb>廿八</rb><rt>nyiæn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>殺掉個</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>兇惡個</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>Yü-to</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>傷了</rb><rt>sông-leh</rt><rb>陣裏個</rb><rt>dzing-li-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>算來</rb><rt>sön-læ</rt><rb>篤篤</rb><rt>toh-toh</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>六七個</rb><rt>loh-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>好點</rb><rt>hao-tin</rt><rb>主顧是</rb><rt>cü-kwu-z</rt><rb>善終個</rb><rt>jün-cong-go</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>loh-væn</rt></ruby> 287 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提賀葛里相</rb><rt>Di-ʽo-keh-li-siang</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>拔上</rb><rt>bah-zông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>do-kô</rt><rb>坐位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>皇帝了</rb><rt>wông-ti-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奧古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>挑選</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爵位</rb><rt>tsiah-we</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>一等</rb><rt>ih-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一國</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奧古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。

<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>貢旦定</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>即位</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>合攏</rb><rt>ʽah-long</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>遷都</rb><rt>tsʽin-tu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>他大尼里</rb><rt>Tʽa-da-nyi-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>築</rb><rt>côh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>貢旦定城</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding-dzing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>均士淡丁</rb><rt>kyüing-z-dæn-ting</rt></ruby></u>。

### <ruby><rb>問個</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>寂納</rb><rt>Dzih-neh</rt><rb>廟</rb><rt>miao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>關個</rb><rt>kwæn-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>開個</rb><rt>kʽæ-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>Koh-veng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>先知</rb><rt>Sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但也理</rb><rt>Dæn-yiæ-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 68 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？196 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？300 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>謀殺</rb><rt>meo-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>善終</rb><rt>jün-cong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>提賀葛里相</rb><rt>Di-ʽo-keh-li-siang</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>貢旦定</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>貢旦定城</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding-dzing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>Tong-pin</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>誰拉</rb><rt>jü-lah</rt><rb>奪去</rb><rt>deh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>西邊</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>分了</rb><rt>feng-leh</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>？

---

## <ruby><rb>第六</rb><rt>Di-Loh</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>頭早仔</rb><rt>deo-tsao-ts</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>厚</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt><rb>爽性</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 美德</sup>

<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>打勒</rb><rt>tang-leh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>越弄</rb><rt>yüih-long</rt><rb>越兇</rb><rt>yüih-hyüong</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>太平個</rb><rt>tʽa-bing-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>真刀</rb><rt>tsing-tao</rt><rb>真槍</rb><rt>tsing-tsʽiang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>人陣</rb><rt>nying-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>認認</rb><rt>nying-nying</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>養間</rb><rt>iang-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>鬥</rb><rt>teo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>男男</rb><rt>nen-nen</rt><rb>女女</rb><rt>nyü-nyü</rt><rb>看個</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>拖喫</rb><rt>tʽa-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>對手</rb><rt>te-siu</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>看東</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>只會</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>摑腳</rb><rt>kwah-kyiah</rt><rb>摑手</rb><rt>kwah-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呵囉大笑</rb><rt>ho-lo-da-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>高興相</rb><rt>kao-hying-siang</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 500 <ruby><rb>多星</rb><rt>to-sing</rt><rb>獅子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大拉象</rb><rt>Da-lah-ziang</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby> 1,0000 <ruby><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby> 1,0000 <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>提了</rb><rt>di-leh</rt><rb>刀槍</rb><rt>tao-tsʽiang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>革老丟</rb><rt>Keh-lao-tiu</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 1,9000 <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>認真話</rb><rt>nying-tsing-wô</rt><rb>像兩國</rb><rt>ziang-liang-koh</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>嬉頭</rb><rt>hyi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>心焦煞了</rb><rt>sing-tsiao-sah-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>tsih-kwun</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>造反</rb><rt>dzao-fæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Trajan，現譯爲<u>圖拉真</u></sup>

<sup>\[2\] Claudius，現譯爲<u>克勞狄</u>。此處爲<u>克勞狄</u>一世</sup>

<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>借過來</rb><rt>tsia-ko-læ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞基薄</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-bôh</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>呆大大</rb><rt>ngæ-do-do</rt><rb>廟宇</rb><rt>miao-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>都神殿</rb><rt>Tu-jing-din</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] Agrippa，現譯爲<u>阿格里帕</u>，羅馬政治家、軍人</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 現譯爲<u>萬神殿</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>Bu-sah</rt><rb>越</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真心</rb><rt>tsing-sing</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>越發</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>缺了</rb><rt>kyʽüih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人只會</rb><rt>nying-tsih-we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>頂壞</rb><rt>ting-w̆a</rt><rb>壞坯子</rb><rt>w̆a-pʽe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>頂壞</rb><rt>ting-w̆a</rt><rb>壞鄉方</rb><rt>w̆a-hyiang-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>流落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>窞裏</rb><rt>den-li</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>壞是</rb><rt>w̆a-z</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>到極頭</rb><rt>tao-gyih-deo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>羅馬書</rb><rt>Lo-mô-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>第一章</rb><rt>di-ih-tsông</rt></ruby>）。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>並弗是</rb><rt>bing-feh-z</rt><rb>弗曉得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就話</rb><rt>ziu-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>聖賢</rb><rt>sing-yin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>做人個</rb><rt>tso-nying-go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聖賢</rb><rt>sing-yin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>M-kao</rt><rb>懼憚</rb><rt>gyü-dæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>嘸法</rb><rt>m-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起來</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>巧勒</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>教人</rb><rt>kao-nying</rt><rb>德行</rb><rt>teh-ʽang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奧古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby> 17 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>以馬努力</rb><rt>Yi-mô-nu-lih</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>」。<ruby><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>Dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四五十</rb><rt>s-ng-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>行教</rb><rt>ʽang-kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>個</rb><rt>Lo-mô-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>已經興旺</rb><rt>yi-kying-hying-wông</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Emmanuel，又譯爲以馬內利。希伯來語，意爲「上帝與我們同在」</sup>

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼羅</rb><rt>Nyi-lo</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>了弗得</rb><rt>liao-feh-teh</rt><rb>兇惡</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>了弗得</rb><rt>liao-feh-teh</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>據</rb><rt>Kyü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>放了</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>燒掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>推撥</rb><rt>tʽe-peh</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>冤枉</rb><rt>ün-wông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>抲來</rb><rt>kʽô-læ</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>信差</rb><rt>sing-tsʽa</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>在其內</rb><rt>dzæ-gyi-nen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>花園</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>蠟燭</rb><rt>lah-coh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Nero，現譯爲<u>尼祿</u>，<u>羅馬</u>皇帝之一</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Apostle，指基督教中爲了某個特殊使命奉召被差派出去的門徒，後譯爲使徒</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>公侯</rb><rt>kong-ʽeo</rt><rb>雖然</rb><rt>se-jün</rt><rb>弗管</rb><rt>feh-kwun</rt><rb>閒賬</rb><rt>ʽæn-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼羅</rb><rt>Nyi-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇個人</rb><rt>keh-go-nying</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>也是</rb><rt>yia-z</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>壞勒</rb><rt>w̆a-leh</rt><rb>嘸天理</rb><rt>m-tʽin-li</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲其</rb><rt>kʽô-gyi</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>萬衆人</rb><rt>væn-cong-nying</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>廿年</rb><rt>Nyiæn-nyin</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>陀密相</rb><rt>Do-mih-siang</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>信差</rb><rt>sing-tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>充軍</rb><rt>cʽong-kyüing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拔摩</rb><rt>Bah-mo</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>海島</rb><rt>hæ-tao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Domitianus，英語化爲 Domitian，現譯爲<u>圖密善</u>。<u>羅馬</u>皇帝之一</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Πάτμος，又譯爲<u>帕特莫斯</u>。海島名，在<u>希臘</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>越</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>越多</rb><rt>yüih-to</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 98 <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大拉象</rb><rt>Da-lah-ziang</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>帝</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>庇推尼</rb><rt>Pi-tʽe-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-tôh</rt><rb>奏文</rb><rt>tseo-veng</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>行勒</rb><rt>ʽang-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>廣</rb><rt>kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>廟宇裏</rb><rt>miao-yü-li</rt><rb>拜菩薩個</rb><rt>pa-bu-sah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 上奏。此處指<u>比提尼亞與本都行省</u>的省長<u>小普林尼</u>和皇帝<u>圖拉真</u>的書信</sup>

「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>鄉方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一般人</rb><rt>ih-pæn-nying</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt><rb>黑早</rb><rt>heh-tsao</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>做𡍲</rb><rt>tso-tʽah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>唱</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt><rb>曲子</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>偷盜</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>失約</rb><rt>shih-iah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐帶攏</rb><rt>zo-tæn-long</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>一廚</rb><rt>ih-djü</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>？」

<ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>其話</rb><rt>gyi-wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>殺個</rb><rt>sah-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>弗少</rb><rt>feh-siao</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 170 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天搖</rb><rt>tʽin-yiao</rt><rb>地動</rb><rt>di-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>動勒</rb><rt>dong-leh</rt><rb>厲害個</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奏文</rb><rt>tseo-veng</rt><rb>皇上</rb><rt>Wông-zông</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>邪教</rb><rt>zia-kyiao</rt><rb>行勒</rb><rt>ʽang-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>廣</rb><rt>kwông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>挑唆</rb><rt>tʽiao-so</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt><rb>做到</rb><rt>tso-tao</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>上諭</rb><rt>zông-yü</rt><rb>批落來</rb><rt>pʽi-lôh-læ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗必</rb><rt>feh-pih</rt><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-leh</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若講</rb><rt>ziah-kông</rt><rb>天搖</rb><rt>tʽin-yiao</rt><rb>地動</rb><rt>di-dong</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>喫𢞕煞</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>安安</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>穩穩</rb><rt>w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djoh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt><rb>嘸過</rb><rt>m-ko</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安敦</rb><rt>En-teng</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] Aurelius，現譯爲<u>奧勒留</u>。<u>羅馬</u>皇帝之一，《後漢書》稱其爲<u>大秦</u>王<u>安敦</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>總而言之</rb><rt>Tsong-r-yin-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby> 300 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>殺掉個</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>數都數弗遍</rb><rt>su-tu-su-feh-pin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>只是介</rb><rt>tsih-z-ka</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>越發</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直等</rb><rt>dzih-teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>貢旦定</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>登位</rb><rt>teng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>入耶穌教</rb><rt>jih-Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>廟宇</rb><rt>miao-yü</rt><rb>拆掉</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>假菩薩</rb><rt>kô-bu-sah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪名個</rb><rt>ze-ming-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從此之後</rb><rt>Dzong-tsʽ-ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>統性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>算是</rb><rt>sön-z</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>lôh-væn</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>弗多</rb><rt>feh-to</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>中國</rb><rt>Cong-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>漢</u>朝</rb><rt>Hen-dziao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>和尚</rb><rt>ʽo-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>漢</rb><rt>Hen</rt><rb>明帝</rb><rt>Ming-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聘請</rb><rt>pʽing-tsʽing</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自從</rb><rt>z-dzong</rt><rb>佛教</rb><rt>Veh-kyiao</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>中國</rb><rt>Cong-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>毛毛</rb><rt>mao-mao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 2000 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>歷朝個</rb><rt>Lih-dziao-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>緊</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>作興</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>佛個</rb><rt>Veh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>三分之一</rb><rt>sæn-feng-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>而並且</rb><rt>r-ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三分</rb><rt>sæn-feng</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>都計</rb><rt>tu-kyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有口</rb><rt>yiu-kʽeo</rt><rb>無心個</rb><rt>vu-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起來</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比弗來個</rb><rt>pi-feh-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>國</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>出個</rb><rt>cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>國</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>小小管</rb><rt>siao-siao-kun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>推扳</rb><rt>tʽe-pæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>鄉方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>別</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>刻忖個</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>統性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>抄滅</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>壞人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>信差</rb><rt>Sing-tsʽa</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>低微</rb><rt>ti-vi</rt><rb>出身個</rb><rt>cʽih-sing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>行教</rb><rt>ʽang-kyiao</rt><rb>一連</rb><rt>ih-lin</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>朝代</rb><rt>dziao-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起來</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>行開去</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>惡像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗得知</rb><rt>feh-teh-cü</rt><rb>只消</rb><rt>tsih-siao</rt></ruby> 300 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>興旺了</rb><rt>hying-wông-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>強橫</rb><rt>gyiang-wang</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>屬國</rb><rt>joh-koh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>前頭個</rb><rt>zin-deo-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>真神</rb><rt>Tsing-Jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>豈難道</rb><rt>kyʽi-næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>武藝子</rb><rt>vu-nyih-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做勒到個</rb><rt>tso-leh-tao-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>釋教</rb><rt>Sih-kyiao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>道教</rb><rt>Dao-kyiao</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>衰敗</rb><rt>sæ-ba</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看起來</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>中國</rb><rt>Cong-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>庉弗得</rb><rt>deng-feh-teh</rt><rb>二三百</rb><rt>nyi-sæn-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 也許；可能</sup>
