### <ruby><rb>問個</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>本來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>壞了</rb><rt>w̆a-de</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>心焦了</rb><rt>Sing-tsiao-de</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>嬉頭</rb><rt>hyi-deo</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>帶累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懂</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>理性</rb><rt>li-sing</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>德行</rb><rt>teh-ʽang</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>奧古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u> 27 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u>？<u><ruby><rb>尼羅</rb><rt>Nyi-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？20 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 98 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？170 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？300 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>耶穌教個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>抲法</rb><rt>kʽô-fah</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>缺點</rb><rt>kyʽüih-tin</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>貢旦定</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>釋教</rb><rt>Sih-kyiao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>行開</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ</rt><rb>比起來</rb><rt>pi-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？

<sup>\[1\] 使得；導致</sup>

## <ruby><rb>第七</rb><rt>Di-Tsʽih</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb>真神</rb><rt>Tsing-Jing</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-liao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>盡頭個</rb><rt>zing-deo-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其該管</rb><rt>gyi-kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>國</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>滅進</rb><rt>mih-tsing</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>屬國</rb><rt>joh-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>統性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>北頭</rb><rt>poh-deo</rt><rb>蠻人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>亂進來</rb><rt>lön-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>統性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>滅進</rb><rt>mih-tsing</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>只會</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>拜服</rb><rt>pa-voh</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>入教</rb><rt>jih-kyiao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>教門</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>越發</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>行開去</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>終於</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歐羅巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奧古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>頂多</rb><rt>ting-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>極好個</rb><rt>gyih-hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>一代</rb><rt>ih-dæ</rt><rb>一代</rb><rt>ih-dæ</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>推扳</rb><rt>tʽe-pæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>北邊</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>蠻人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>亂進個</rb><rt>lön-tsing-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀書</rb><rt>doh-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>原是</rb><rt>Nyün-z</rt><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖經</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>行開去</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>變做</rb><rt>pin-tso</rt><rb>光明</rb><rt>kwông-ming</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>印版</rb><rt>ing-pæn</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。《<ruby><rb>聖經</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>抄本</rb><rt>tsʽao-peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>讀弗出</rb><rt>doh-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>話弗來</rb><rt>wô-feh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>邪開去</rb><rt>zia-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教頭</rb><rt>kyiao-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>哄騙</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>差朵</rb><rt>tsʽô-tô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>馬太傳</rb><rt>Mô-tʽæ-djün</rt></ruby>》XVI，16–19 <ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>–</rt><rb>說錯</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>穌</u>封</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>得</u>到</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt><rb>世</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>間上傳</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>公</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>會上</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>頭。<u>彼</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>得</u>死</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt><rb>後，箇</rb><rt>si-ʽeo</rt><rb>個權</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>柄又</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>落撥</rb><rt>djün-lôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>馬</u>教</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt><rb>頭了</rb><rt>kyiao-deo</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>教頭</rb><rt>kyiao-deo</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>辦事體</rb><rt>bæn-z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>樣色</rb><rt>yiang-seh</rt><rb>弗會</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>弄錯</rb><rt>long-tsʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖口</rb><rt>sing-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>數</rb><rt>su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>鐵色無二個</rb><rt>tʽih-seh-vu-r-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>Pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懼憚</rb><rt>gyü-dæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>旗下人</rb><rt>Gyi-ʽô-nying</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>喇嘛僧</rb><rt>La-mô-seng</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教頭</rb><rt>kyiao-deo</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>弄權</rb><rt>long-gyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>邪</rb><rt>zia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>地步</rb><rt>di-bu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>本來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖經</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>笨個</rb><rt>beng-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖經</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>一明白</rb><rt>ih-ming-bah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt><rb>鄉方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt><rb>越弄</rb><rt>yüih-long</rt><rb>越弗是</rb><rt>yüih-feh-z</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>經</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>且顧</rb><rt>tsʽia-kwu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>庵堂</rb><rt>en-dông</rt><rb>寺院裏</rb><rt>z-yün-li</rt><rb>修行</rb><rt>siu-ʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>死過了</rb><rt>si-ko-liao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>懺唸</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所害</rb><rt>sô-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>庉到</rb><rt>deng-tao</rt><rb>毛毛</rb><rt>mao-mao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 1000 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>黑暗個</rb><rt>heh-en-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>天主教</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>錯處</rb><rt>tsʽo-cʽü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby> 1000 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>發出來</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>裏頭個</rb><rt>li-deo-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>弗必</rb><rt>feh-pih</rt><rb>說</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>缺個</rb><rt>kyʽüih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一兩樣</rb><rt>ih-liang-yiang</rt><rb>要緊點</rb><rt>iao-kying-tin</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>講了相似</rb><rt>kông-leh-siang-z</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>嘸幾百年</rb><rt>m-kyi-pah-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真道理</rb><rt>tsing-dao-li</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>變掉</rb><rt>pin-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>獨獨</rb><rt>doh-doh</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>真神</rb><rt>Tsing-Jing</rt><rb>弗夠</rb><rt>feh-keo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>死過了</rb><rt>si-ko-liao</rt><rb>聖人</rb><rt>sing-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>受難</rb><rt>ziu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗夠</rb><rt>feh-keo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>虛花</rb><rt>hyü-hwô</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>補補湊</rb><rt>pu-pu-tsʽeo</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>。

<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>國</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>出身</rb><rt>cʽih-sing</rt><rb>之地</rb><rt>ts-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>罪名個</rb><rt>ze-ming-go</rt><rb>功勞</rb><rt>kong-lao</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>足足個</rb><rt>tsoh-tsoh-go</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下世個</rb><rt>ʽô-si-go</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算數個</rb><rt>sön-su-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>虔心個</rb><rt>gyin-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>往往</rb><rt>w̆ông-w̆ông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>特特</rb><rt>deh-deh</rt><rb>爲爲</rb><rt>we-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>墳</rb><rt>veng</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>lôh-væn</rt></ruby> 1055 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>土耳基</rb><rt>Tʽu-r-kyi</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>回子</rb><rt>We-ts</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>國</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照咋</rb><rt>tsiao-dza</rt><rb>難爲</rb><rt>næn-we</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>歐羅巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>方</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>攏總怏煞</rb><rt>long-tsong-ông-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>救主個</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü-go</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>弗相信</rb><rt>feh-siang-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>佔住間</rb><rt>tsin-djü-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶穌教個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao-go</rt><rb>霉</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>起了</rb><rt>kyʽi-leh</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>個打個</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赦</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 現譯爲<u>土耳其</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>歸根</rb><rt>Kwe-keng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>特買</rb><rt>deh-ma</rt><rb>國個</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>合帶攏</rb><rt>ʽeh-tæn-long</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>回回教</rb><rt>We-we-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt><rb>穌</rb><rt>su</rt></ruby></u> 1096 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>三年</rb><rt>sæn-nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>破進</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>傷勒</rb><rt>sông-leh</rt><rb>陣裏個</rb><rt>dzing-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>算起來</rb><rt>sön-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 85,0000。

<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby> 80 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>國</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>該管個</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>回子</rb><rt>We-ts</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>強起來</rb><rt>gyiang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>奪了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>歐羅巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各處</rb><rt>Kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>擔綱</rb><rt>tæn-kông</rt><rb>起兵</rb><rt>kyʽi-ping</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>回子</rb><rt>We-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救轉</rb><rt>kyiu-cün</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>Veng-mo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>打上</rb><rt>tang-zông</rt><rb>一百多</rb><rt>ih-pah-to</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>冷</rb><rt>lang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>纔始</rb><rt>dzæ-s</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>歇手</rb><rt>hyih-siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傷傷掉個</rb><rt>sông-sông-diao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>足足</rb><rt>tsoh-tsoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>頭兩</rb><rt>deo-liang</rt><rb>百萬</rb><rt>pah-væn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>回子</rb><rt>We-ts</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算進</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>一口氣</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo-kyʽi</rt><rb>颬勒</rb><rt>hô-leh</rt><rb>壁裏</rb><rt>pih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>牽</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>空磨</rb><rt>kʽong-mo</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>蝕煞</rb><rt>zih-sah</rt><rb>老本個</rb><rt>lao-peng-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>邪教</rb><rt>zia-kyiao</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>黑暗時世</rb><rt>Heh-en-z-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 白費力氣</sup>

<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>第二樣</rb><rt>di-nyi-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做根脈<sup>\[1\]</sup>個</rb><rt>tso-keng-mah-go</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>虧了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>造反</rb><rt>dzao-fæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 源頭</sup>

<ruby><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>Tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都計</rb><rt>tu-kyi</rt><rb>弗依</rb><rt>feh-i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>別國個</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>帶領</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>和衆</rb><rt>ʽo-cong</rt><rb>抄滅</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>嘸幾個</rb><rt>m-kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>兵馬</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>用弗着</rb><rt>yüong-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>直等</rb><rt>dzih-teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 1204 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>忖出</rb><rt>tsʽeng-cʽih</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>禁</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>各處</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一進</rb><rt>ih-tsing</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天牢</rb><rt>tʽin-lao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>Dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>衙門</rb><rt>ngô-meng</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>封</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>銜頭</rb><rt>ngæn-deo</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>查察官</rb><rt>Dzô-tsʽah-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>Dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>衙門</rb><rt>ngô-meng</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>半句</rb><rt>pun-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講錯</rb><rt>kông-tsʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>眼眼</rb><rt>ngæn-ngæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>弗對</rb><rt>feh-te</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>查察官</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah-kwun</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連即</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>獨門</rb><rt>doh-meng</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>屈打</rb><rt>kyʽüih-tang</rt><rb>成招</rb><rt>dzing-tsiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>死罪</rb><rt>Si-ze</rt><rb>定落</rb><rt>ding-lôh</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>頂苦個</rb><rt>ting-kwʽu-go</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>周作</rb><rt>tsiu-tsôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抲了去</rb><rt>kʽô-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連即</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>頭緒</rb><rt>deo-jü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>M-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗敢</rb><rt>feh-ken</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>一響</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>自家</rb><rt>zi-kô</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>難保</rb><rt>næn-pao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Inquisition，現譯爲宗教裁判所</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 頭銜。現代寧波話就叫「頭銜」</sup>

<sup>\[3\] 僅僅；單單</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>查察官</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah-kwun</rt><rb>銜頭</rb><rt>ngæn-deo</rt><rb>雖然</rb><rt>se-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>聖人」個</rb><rt>sing-nying-go</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>聖」字</rb><rt>sing-z</rt><rb>加上</rb><rt>kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>聖查察官</rb><rt>sing-dzô-tsʽah-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>老虎</rb><rt>Lao-hwu</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>好看點</rb><rt>hao-kʽen-tin</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>無冤</rb><rt>vu-ün</rt><rb>無故</rb><rt>vu-kwu</rt><rb>冤枉</rb><rt>ün-wông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拼空</rb><rt>pʽing-kʽong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抲了去</rb><rt>kʽô-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>查察衙門</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah-ngô-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天主教</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>直等</rb><rt>Dzih-teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>lôh-væn</rt></ruby> 1500 <ruby><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>歐羅巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>教門</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt><rb>邪</rb><rt>zia</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖經</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>歸正</rb><rt>Kwe-tsing</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>地理</rb><rt>Di-li</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>》53、54 <ruby><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>）。

<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歐羅巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>天主教個</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有<u>比利時</u></rb><rt>tsih-yiu-Pi-li-z</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>佛蘭西</rb><rt>Veh-læn-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西班牙</rb><rt>Si-pæn-yüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>葡萄牙</rb><rt>Bu-dao-yüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>奧地利亞</rb><rt>Ao-di-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>還有星</rb><rt>wa-yiu-sing</rt><rb>雜箇愣敦</rb><rt>zeh-keh-leng-teng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>虧了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>別烏碎</rb><rt>bih-u-sen</rt><rb>歸正個</rb><rt>kwe-tsing-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>有點</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>借過來</rb><rt>tsia-ko-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>教皇個</rb><rt>kyiao-wông-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>推扳</rb><rt>tʽe-pæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>本來</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>興起來個</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖經</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>行開去</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>帖撒羅尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby>》II, 8）。

### <ruby><rb>問個</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>國</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>北頭</rb><rt>Poh-deo</rt><rb>蠻人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>耶穌教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>幾時</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>壞了</rb><rt>w̆a-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>弗轉</rb><rt>feh-cün</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主教</rb><rt>cü-kyiao</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>暗了</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>天主教</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao</rt><rb>錯處</rb><rt>tsʽo-cʽü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>起頭個</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>Deo</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>得</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>仔細</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>話帶來</rb><rt>wô-tæn-læ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>樣</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話勒</rb><rt>wô-leh</rt><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>Dza</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>歸正</rb><rt>Kwe-tsing</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>天主教</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>嘸得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>？《<ruby><rb>帖撒羅尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話的</rb><rt>wô-tih</rt></ruby>？

## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>八</rb><rt>Pah</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb>自從</rb><rt>Z-dzong</rt><rb>北頭</rb><rt>Poh-deo</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>蠻人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>分了</rb><rt>feng-leh</rt><rb>特買</rb><rt>deh-ma</rt><rb>國生</rb><rt>koh-sang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>同心</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>協力</rb><rt>yiah-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>節制</rb><rt>tsih-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>日耳曼</rb><rt>Jih-r-mæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>使喚</rb><rt>s-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>佛蘭西</rb><rt>Veh-læn-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奧地利亞</rb><rt>Ao-di-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有點</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天理</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>循環</rb><rt>jing-wæn</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天下個</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>早已仔</rb><rt>tsao-yi-ts</rt><rb>喫盡</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>小苦</rb><rt>siao-kwʽu</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>還報</rb><rt>wæn-pao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>三四百年</rb><rt>Sæn-s-pah-nyin</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>別</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>鬧熱</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀書</rb><rt>doh-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>歸正了</rb><rt>kwe-tsing-liao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>只會</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>好起來</rb><rt>hao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>差遠</rb><rt>tsʽô-yün</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>活被</rb><rt>weh-be</rt><rb>邪教</rb><rt>zia-kyiao</rt><rb>所害</rb><rt>sô-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希臘</u>國</rb><rt>Hyi-lah-koh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗上</rb><rt>feh-zông</rt><rb>弗落個</rb><rt>feh-lôh-go</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>庵堂</rb><rt>en-dông</rt><rb>寺院</rb><rt>z-yün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>鬧熱</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和尚</rb><rt>ʽo-zông</rt><rb>尼姑</rb><rt>nyi-kwu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>聖經</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>把戲</rb><rt>pô-hyi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做出來</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>鄉方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt><rb>雖然</rb><rt>se-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>古時</rb><rt>kwu-z</rt><rb>節</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>兇惡</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>真神個</rb><rt>Tsing-Jing-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>假個</rb><rt>kô-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>統</rb><rt>Tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>分了</rb><rt>feng-leh</rt><rb>十國生</rb><rt>jih-koh-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>多斯加納</rb><rt>To-s-kyüô-neh</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>米蘭</rb><rt>Mi-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒爾的尼亞</rb><rt>Sah-r-tih-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>摩納哥</rb><rt>Mo-neh-ko</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴爾馬</rb><rt>Pô-r-mô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[3\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>摩德拿</rb><rt>Mo-teh-nô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[4\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>盧加</rb><rt>Lu-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[5\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>聖馬利涅</rb><rt>Sing-Mô-li-nyiah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[6\]</sup><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十𡍲</rb><rt>jih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby><sup>\[7\]</sup>。

<sup>\[2\] Sardegna，現譯爲<u>撒丁</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十國</rb><rt>jih-koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>統性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby> 12,0000 <ruby><rb>轉方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>戶口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 2250,0000。<ruby><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>轉方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 188 <ruby><rb>介相貌</rb><rt>ka-siang-mao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 平方英里，1 平方英里約等於 2.59 平方公里</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天家</rb><rt>tʽin-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>和暖</rb><rt>ʽo-nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>厚</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>世口</rb><rt>shü-kʽeo</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>便當</rb><rt>bin-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>種田</rb><rt>cong-din</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做生意</rb><rt>tso-sang-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出息</rb><rt>cʽih-sih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>怕弗</rb><rt>pʽô-feh</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕弗</rb><rt>pʽô-feh</rt><rb>富足起來</rb><rt>fu-tsoh-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>十分</rb><rt>jih-feng</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 時代</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>資質</rb><rt>ts-tsih</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>教門</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt><rb>邪</rb><rt>zia</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>讀書</rb><rt>doh-shü</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>個數個</rb><rt>ko-su-go</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>山水</rb><rt>sæn-se</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>天生成個</rb><rt>tʽin-sang-dzing-go</rt><rb>秀緻</rb><rt>siu-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>西邊</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>隨爾</rb><rt>ze-ng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>比弗來個</rb><rt>pi-feh-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>丹青</rb><rt>tæn-tsʽing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>篤石頭案</rb><rt>tôh-zah-deo-en</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>細巧</rb><rt>si-kyʽiao</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>頂好</rb><rt>ting-hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>別國</rb><rt>Bih-koh</rt><rb>有名頭</rb><rt>yiu-ming-deo</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>學來</rb><rt>ʽôh-læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做詩個</rb><rt>tso-s-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗少</rb><rt>feh-siao</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>自從</rb><rt>Z-dzong</rt><rb>北頭</rb><rt>Poh-deo</rt><rb>蠻人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>亂進</rb><rt>lön-tsing</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>咋個了</rb><rt>dza-go-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>三四</rb><rt>Sæn-s</rt><rb>百</rb><rt>pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>讀書</rb><rt>doh-shü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>比起來</rb><rt>pi-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>現在呢</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ-ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>Soh-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>鄉方</rb><rt>Hyiang-fông</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>Soh-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>分了</rb><rt>feng-leh</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>國生</rb><rt>koh-sang</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>Koh-veng</rt><rb>統性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>咋管</rb><rt>dza-kun</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>統性</rb><rt>Tʽong-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>戶口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>一塊</rb><rt>Ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>轉方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>生成</rb><rt>sang-dzing</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>讀書</rb><rt>Doh-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>細巧</rb><rt>si-kyʽiao</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>？

## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>Kyiu</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

1\. <ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-kôh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 1,7200 <ruby><rb>轉方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戶口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 273,0000。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>該管個</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>國家個</rb><rt>Koh-kô-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>教門裏</rb><rt>kyiao-meng-li</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>辦個</rb><rt>bæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作興</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>公事</rb><rt>kong-z</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>印出</rb><rt>ing-cʽih</rt><rb>報</rb><rt>Pao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>生意</rb><rt>Sang-i</rt><rb>冷靜</rb><rt>lang-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>討飯</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt><rb>惡多</rb><rt>ôh-to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大概</rb><rt>Da-kæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十國</rb><rt>jih-koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>末等</rb><rt>meh-teng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>京城</rb><rt>Kying-dzing</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>坐來</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt><rb>默示錄</rb><rt>mah-z-loh</rt></ruby>》XVII）。<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>當初子</rb><rt>tông-tsʽu-ts</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五六十萬</rb><rt>ng-loh-jih-væn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>巴巴</rb><rt>pô-pô</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>十四五萬</rb><rt>jih-s-ng-væn</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>庵堂</rb><rt>en-dông</rt><rb>寺院</rb><rt>z-yün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>男女</rb><rt>nen-nyü</rt><rb>弗作</rb><rt>feh-tsôh</rt><rb>成親</rb><rt>dzing-tsʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>城裏向</rb><rt>dzing-li-hyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>水土</rb><rt>shü-tʽu</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>顧</rb><rt>kwu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>虧了</rb><rt>Kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>別烏碎</rb><rt>bih-u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>常</rb><rt>djông</rt><rb>常</rb><rt>djông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>日久之長</rb><rt>nyih-kyiu-ts-dziang</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>空城</rb><rt>kʽong-dzing</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>古時節個</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin-go</rt><rb>皇宮</rb><rt>wông-kong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>衙門</rb><rt>ngô-meng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>廟宇</rb><rt>miao-yü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>古蹟</rb><rt>kwu-tsih</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>也有</rb><rt>Yia-yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>禮拜堂</rb><rt>li-pa-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扎括</rb><rt>tsah-kwah</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>勒</rb><rt>leh</rt><rb>華華</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>麗麗</rb><rt>li-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>精緻</rb><rt>tsing-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>禮拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>堂</rb><rt>dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>彼得堂</rb><rt>Pe-teh-dông</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>動工</rb><rt>dong-kong</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt><rb>爲止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 117 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby> 1600,00000 <ruby><rb>塊</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt><rb>番餅</rb><rt>fæn-ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>教皇</rb><rt>Kyiao-wông</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>弗夠</rb><rt>feh-keo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>作出</rb><rt>tsôh-cʽih</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>關牒</rb><rt>kwæn-diah</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>關牒</rb><rt>kwæn-diah</rt><rb>請去間</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyʽi-kæn</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-Wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 裝飾</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 聖<u>伯多祿</u>大殿，或稱聖<u>彼得</u>大教堂</sup>

<sup>\[3\] 證書。此處指<u>羅馬</u>教廷發行的大赦證明書，即後世所謂贖罪券</sup>

2\. <u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西基里</rb><rt>Si-kyi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>算進</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 4,2000 <ruby><rb>轉方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戶口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 796,0000。<ruby><rb>京城</rb><rt>Kying-dzing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>虧了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-kôh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>載來</rb><rt>tsæ-læ</rt><rb>載去</rb><rt>tsæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>便</rb><rt>bin</rt><rb>鬧熱個</rb><rt>nao-nyih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>一眼都</rb><rt>ih-ngæn-tu</rt><rb>嘸做</rb><rt>m-tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>過海</rb><rt>ko-hæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt><rb>惡多</rb><rt>ôh-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>震勒</rb><rt>cing-leh</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>房子</rb><rt>vông-ts</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>無千大萬</rb><rt>vu-tsʽin-da-væn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>壓煞</rb><rt>ah-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤈦起來</rb><rt>me-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一滿</rb><rt>ih-mun</rt><rb>壓弗住</rb><rt>ah-feh-djü</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>震動</rb><rt>cing-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>震勒</rb><rt>cing-leh</rt><rb>岔</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>統</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>外鄉</rb><rt>nga-hyiang</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>火山</rb><rt>ho-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>空心個</rb><rt>kʽong-sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下頭</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>煬開了</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-liao</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>煬開了個</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-liao-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>流個</rb><rt>liu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>喇𠲎</rb><rt>La-va</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爆出來</rb><rt>pao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>流落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>溪坑</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>式勢</rb><rt>sih-shü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>厲害個</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] lava，現稱爲熔岩</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>有名頭</rb><rt>yiu-ming-deo</rt><rb>火山</rb><rt>ho-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>維蘇維</rb><rt>Vi-su-vi</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爆出來</rb><rt>pao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。140 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山腳下</rb><rt>sæn-kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>古蹟</rb><rt>kwu-tsih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掘出來</rb><rt>gyüih-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>瓦爿</rb><rt>ngô-bæn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>爛泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>起過</rb><rt>kyʽi-ko</rt><rb>一邊</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>露出來</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>上好斯樣<sup>\[2\]</sup>個</rb><rt>zông-hao-s-nyiang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>庉晌</rb><rt>Deng-zông</rt></ruby> 40 6\. <u><ruby><rb>摩納哥</rb><rt>Mo-neh-ko</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby> 52 <ruby><rb>轉方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戶口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby> 7000 <ruby><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有一個</rb><rt>yiu-ih-go</rt><rb>國王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>全靠</rb><rt>jün-kʽao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒爾的尼亞</rb><rt>Sah-r-tih-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>保護</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文錯印爲 mô-neh-ko</sup>

### <ruby><rb>問個</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>戶口</rb><rt>Wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>服</rb><rt>Voh</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>公事</rb><rt>Kong-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>Pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb><u>羅馬</u>國</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>比起來</rb><rt>pi-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>羅馬城</rb><rt>Lo-mô-dzing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>建來</rb><rt>kyin-læ</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>？《<ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt><rb>默示錄</rb><rt>Meh-z-loh</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>點着</rb><rt>tin-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>古時節</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>古蹟</rb><rt>kwu-tsih</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>禮拜堂</rb><rt>Li-pa-dông</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>彼得堂</rb><rt>Pe-teh-dông</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話的</rb><rt>wô-tih</rt></ruby>？

<u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>戶口</rb><rt>Wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>生意</rb><rt>Sang-i</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>地震</rb><rt>Di-cing</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>地震</rb><rt>Di-cing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>講究</rb><rt>kông-kyiu</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>維蘇維</rb><rt>Vi-su-vi</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>山腳下</rb><rt>Sæn-kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>古蹟</rb><rt>kwu-tsih</rt><rb>掘出來</rb><rt>gyüih-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以大拿</rb><rt>Yi-da-nô</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>斯當婆里</rb><rt>S-tông-bo-li</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多斯加納</rb><rt>To-s-kyüô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>話的</rb><rt>wô-tih</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>米蘭</rb><rt>Mi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>韋內色</rb><rt>We-ne-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>德利士底</rb><rt>Teh-li-z-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>撒爾的尼亞</rb><rt>Sah-r-tih-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>別處</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>摩納哥</rb><rt>Mo-neh-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>巴爾馬</rb><rt>Pô-r-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>盧加</rb><rt>Lu-Kyüô</rt></ruby></u>？<u><ruby><rb>摩德拿</rb><rt>Mo-teh-nô</rt></ruby></u>？<u><ruby><rb>聖馬利涅</rb><rt>Sing-Mô-li-nyiah</rt></ruby></u>？
